  p1: 터미널 핑퐁게임 만들어줘

# plan: main-opus (Part A)
status: GATED - apply only after user confirms (conflicts with DECISIONS.md:47 2026-10-31 "haiku main takes user input directly ... settings.json = haiku")
user_verbatim: "추천대로 진행하고, ..." (recommendation text not in notes; relayed by Haiku main as: default main -> Opus; Haiku only relays input and translates output)
goal: default main model = opus; Haiku role = input relay + output translation (haiku-tui) only; haiku-쓰기용 = haiku-batch-worker / haiku-coder; haiku-읽기용 = haiku-scout / haiku-researcher; no new agents
why_gated: CLAUDE.md and .claude/settings.json are config; change needs the user's own confirmation, not an agent relay

## steps (haiku-coder, in order)
1. internal/harness/repl.go:1186 `fs.StringVar(&a.Model, "model", "haiku", "")` -> "opus"
2. internal/harness/core.go:139-140 `if o.Model == "" { o.Model = "haiku" }` -> "opus"

4. keep: orch.go:23-31 (e -> haiku, b/c/d/f -> opus), resident.go:28-29 fixed=opus, resident.go:42-43 min=haiku, `--model haiku` still works
5. .claude/settings.json:2 "model": "haiku" -> "opus"
6. CLAUDE.md:1,7,10 rewrite to: main = Opus (default); Haiku main only when `--model haiku`, then it relays input to opus-architect and haiku-tui translates output; write agents = haiku-batch-worker/haiku-coder (haiku-쓰기용), read agents = haiku-scout/haiku-researcher (haiku-읽기용)
7. plan/caveman-structure.md: add the same role mapping line (haiku-쓰기용 / haiku-읽기용) where agent roles are listed
8. notes/DECISIONS.md append: `[model-policy] 2026-11-01 user "추천대로 진행": default main = opus; supersedes 2026-10-31 entry (line 47)`

## acceptance

- grep -n '"haiku"' repl.go core.go -> no default-model hits left (orch/resident excepted)
- grep "Haiku main" CLAUDE.md -> only in the --model haiku clause

task: install fails on Windows (PowerShell and Git Bash) and bash; retry on this Mac
release: v0.9.13 latest, assets darwin/linux amd64+arm64 tar.gz, windows amd64+arm64 zip, checksums.txt present (verified)

  error: sudo: a terminal is required to read the password; either use the -S option to read from standard input or configure an askpass helper
  cause: /usr/local/bin is root:wheel 755, not writable by user; no tty for sudo
mac run (CHAUDE_TEST_ROOT=/tmp/chaude-root, no sudo): PASS, chaude 0.9.13
root cause 1 (PowerShell): install.ps1 Save-ReleaseAsset uses .NET HttpClient without User-Agent; GitHub API returns 403 without UA (curl -H 'User-Agent:' -> 403, with UA -> 302). Expected error: "Response status code does not indicate success: 403 (Forbidden)"
root cause 2 (Git Bash/MSYS): install.sh case mingw*|msys*|cygwin* exits "error: Windows is not handled here"
fixes: install.ps1 adds UserAgent 'chaude-installer'; install.sh hands off to powershell.exe with Tls12 + irm install.ps1 | iex

<!-- notes-gc: notes/runs/261102-effort-medium.md (mtime 2026-11-02) -->
# effort 비교: unset / low / medium (메인 Opus, claude -p)

key: date: 2026-11-02
key: commit: 87bb1d32e0b4dc6eb435fe1b34a07bb98e994c0f (기본 medium 변경, .claude/settings.json)
key: model: claude-opus-5-5 (claude 2.1.316)
key: prompt: "Compute 17*23 + 9*8. Reply with only the integer." (expected 463)
key: effort-mechanism: chaude는 --effort 플래그를 넘기지 않음 (internal/harness/core.go sessionArgs에 effort 없음). 프로젝트 cwd의 .claude/settings.json effortLevel이 메인 effort를 정함. 실험은 temp dir의 .claude/settings.json에 effortLevel 주입 (unset = 키 없음 `{}`).
key: cmd: (cd <tmp>/<level> && claude -p "<prompt>" --model claude-opus-5-5 --output-format json --setting-sources project)
key: setting-sources-project-only: 사용자 레벨 ~/.claude/settings.json에 effortLevel "medium"이 있어 unset이 medium으로 섞이지 않도록 project만 로드. chaude 메인 실행과 다른 점.
key: tmp-base: /tmp/effort-bench.hVMahc (temp dir별로 cwd가 달라 system prompt prefix가 달라 캐시가 분리됨)

## 결과 (run별)

| level | run | cost_usd | total_tokens | duration_ms | answer | cache_creation | cache_read | output | thinking |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| unset | 1 | 0.121740 | 15214 | 1705 | 463 | 15209 | 0 | 3 | 0 |
| unset | 2 | 0.003110 | 15214 | 2498 | 463 | 0 | 15209 | 3 | 0 |
| low | 1 | 0.051999 | 15211 | 2789 | 463 | 6268 | 8938 | 3 | 0 |
| low | 2 | 0.003109 | 15211 | 1616 | 463 | 0 | 15206 | 3 | 0 |
| medium | 1 | 0.052024 | 15214 | 2765 | 463 | 6271 | 8938 | 3 | 0 |
| medium | 2 | 0.003110 | 15214 | 1581 | 463 | 0 | 15209 | 3 | 0 |

total_tokens = input + cache_creation + cache_read + output (input 2 in every run).

## 평균 (n=2 per level)

| level | avg cost_usd | avg total_tokens | avg duration_ms | correct |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| unset | 0.0624 | 15214 | 2101.5 | 2/2 |
| low | 0.0276 | 15211 | 2202.5 | 2/2 |
| medium | 0.0276 | 15214 | 2173.0 | 2/2 |

주의: run 1은 캐시 생성(쓰기) 비용이 들어 비싸고, run 2는 캐시 읽기라 $0.003대. 평균은 캐시 상태에 지배됨. 세 레벨 모두 run 1이 cache write였던 것은 레벨별 temp dir이 달라 prefix가 새로 만들어진 탓이며 effort 효과가 아님.
주의: 출력 3 토큰, thinking 0 (모든 run). 이 프롬프트에서는 effort가 출력/추론 토큰에 드러나지 않음. effort가 실제 적용됐는지 이 실험으로는 증명 안 됨.

## smoke: 빌드된 chaude

- cmd: cd src/main/intt && go build -o <tmp>/chaude-smoke ./cmd/chaude -> BUILD_OK
- cmd: chaude-smoke gen-settings {mac,linux,windows} -> 세 OS 모두 "effortLevel": "medium"
- sessionArgs (core.go) effort 플래그: 없음 (grep 0건). 메인 effort는 settings.json에서 옴.
- TUI 실제 기동(pty)은 하지 않음.

task: output pane link/path coloring broken across wrap, Korean particles, ~ paths, SGR reset (repeat report)
status: done (no commit)
root_cause:
  - linkify ran per wrapped line (wrapLinked -> wrap -> linkify), so a token split by wrap was matched only on each fragment; fragment without "/" (links.go:19 tail "links.go:12") never matched -> color stopped mid-token
  - URL class [^\s<>"'`]+ accepted non-ASCII -> "https://x.com/a에서" and "。" were colored
  - linkWinRe same non-ASCII problem
  - linkDotRe had no "~/" start -> "~/dir/sub" uncolored
  - SGR reset (\x1b[0m) inside a link was copied verbatim and ended the color for the rest of the link
changed:
  - src/main/intt/internal/harness/links.go (linkSpans/markLinks/restyle/wrapLinked; regex classes; linkSpan has url bool)
  - src/main/intt/internal/harness/links_wrap_test.go (new; table test TestWrapLinkedColorsWholeTokens + SGR replay helper)
fix:
  - links found on whole logical line before wrap; marked with zero-width CSI marks (\x1b[9001m URL, \x1b[9002m path, \x1b[9003m end)
  - restyle() converts marks per wrapped line: close at line end, reopen next line, re-apply link style after any inner SGR
  - URL/window class excludes U+0080+ (Korean particles, 。); "~/" added to linkDotRe; "#L12" line anchor suffix on relative paths
checks:
  - reproduced first: 7 subcases FAIL on old code (wrapped URL -> "https://example", "s/links.go:12", particle, 。, ~/dir, reset)
  - go build ./... -> rc 0
  - go vet ./internal/harness/ -> rc 0
  - go test -count=1 ./... -> ok chaude/internal/{clean,harness,scripts,settings,update}
doubts:
  - visual only: underline/color rendering in a real terminal, and whether lipgloss padding on wrapped lines gets underlined
  - Windows paths with Hangul folder names now colored only up to the first Hangul char
  - paths with ( ) # = & in names still not matched (kept prose negatives safe); URLs keep ASCII-only
  - "(url)" with balanced parens: trailing ")" still trimmed

task: agent list shows only one row after resume
scope: crates/cli/src/tui/bar.rs
root_cause: bar top row joins all running agents as one line and clip() cuts it to terminal width; a long first agent (TUI A) pushes the resumed agent off the row. Go draws one row per agent (tui.go bgLines/rowsA).
fix: when agents do not fit, each description is cut to an equal share of the room; every agent keeps a segment.
test: tui::bar::tests::every_running_agent_stays_visible_when_row_is_narrow

date: 2026-11-04
branch: rust-port
scope: crates/core only (cli/tui owned by another coder)
go_truth: deprecated/src/main/intt/internal/harness/ (idlesweep.go, exit_handoff.go, resident coder)

## commits (local, not pushed)
- 0e5932b feat(core): resident coder slots with stage rows and CoderReply (api.rs, resident.rs)
- ccde40c feat(core): project settings write haiku agents and Agent|Task guard (settings/project.rs, settings/hooks/main-thread-guard.py, settings/agents/)
- this run: coder idle expiry, exit handoff, coder/idle/handoff engine tests, doc comment fix (api.rs, tests/engine.rs, this note)

## done
- prompter run: done before this run (earlier commits)
- resident coder slots: done (Cmd::Coder, extra_coders 0-8, CODER_TIMEOUT 30 min, Stage Working/Done/Failed, CoderReply)
- coder test: done (tests/engine.rs coder_task_emits_working_done_rows_and_reply)
- N14 idle expiry for extra coders: done. Config.coder_idle_ttl default 270 s. Base coder (slot 0) never expires (Go isExtraCoder branch). Wakes through wait_budget, swept in on_quiet.
- exit handoff writer: done. Config.handoff_path (default None). Written after the actor loop, before children are killed (covers Cmd::Shutdown and handle disconnect). Fields: version 1, cwd, main_session (omitted when empty), saved_at_unix.
- settings port (project.rs, agents, hook): committed earlier (ccde40c)
- api.rs misplaced doc comment (coder_role between agent_slot's comments): fixed

## remaining
- resident session ids in handoff: prompter/tui/coder session ids are not recorded (only main_session). Go resident_sessions and owned_sessions are empty.
- handoff read side: Go readExitHandoff (used by /view and /resume on next start) not ported.
- handoff timestamp: saved_at_unix (seconds) instead of Go RFC 3339 time. Change only if a reader needs RFC 3339.
- coder as MCP tool (Go resident_mcp.go): not ported. Rust exposes EngineHandle::coder(task).
- idlesweep main-child parts (childDead, cold cache rotation): not ported. Only the extra coder expiry is.

## gates
- cargo build -p chaude-core -> Finished, no warnings
- cargo test -p chaude-core -> lib 67 passed, engine 42 passed, 0 failed (engine ~40 s)
- cargo fmt -p chaude-core -- --check -> clean (after fmt on crate)
- new engine tests: coder_task_emits_working_done_rows_and_reply, idle_extra_coder_expires_and_base_coder_stays (pid-based, uses kill -0), exit_handoff_records_main_session_on_shutdown

## risks
- idle expiry test relies on kill -0 on a reaped pid after SIGKILL; pid tag is the task name (first = base), so it is deterministic. A flaky 5 s wait would show as "extra coder not expired".
- fake coder sleeps 0.2 s before reply; the back-to-back submit depends on slot 0 still busy at the second submit. Verified passing; no retry added.
- .claude/hooks/main-thread-guard.py (+12 lines) remains uncommitted: outside scope, needs confirmation.

# 261104 haiku core-a api items

task: core A workstream (crates/core api.rs, tests/engine.rs). Items 1-5 from coordinator brief.
branch: rust-port

## items
- item1 orch Router route/record per Go: NOT DONE, not wired.
  finding: Go routeOrch has no production caller (grep deprecated/ shows only tests), so wiring Router live diverges from Go. Left unwired. Coordinator decision needed.
  file: crates/core/src/orch.rs untouched (B module).
- item2 replace rotate_deferred with rotate_defer::Deferral: DONE, commit ee03c3e.
  design: Deferral has no clear(); clear_deferral uses hold(0, "", now, ZERO).
  deviation from Go: stale start avoided by clear_deferral when rotation not wanted.
  idle wait polls DEFER_POLL (1 s) because the hold start is private to Deferral.
- item3 AgentInfo started/done/result_done for bg_handoff_lines: DONE, commit 7dfc5df.
  result_done is set false everywhere: agentresult port not done, so a finished agent never lists in the handoff.
- item4 engine test robustness: DONE (wait bound), commit b6624ec.
  change: expect-style waits and wait_for use const WAIT = 60 s (was 10 s). Drain loop and elapsed<10 s assert unchanged.
  root cause: NOT CONFIRMED. Could not reproduce the flake.
  evidence: earlier, 10 busy loops: the four tests passed 3 times. Full engine binary under 12 busy loops: 60/60 passed twice.
- item5 install_import API: DONE, commit a03ae45.
  signature: pub fn EngineHandle::install_import(&self, imp: ClaudeImport)
  caller: let (_, imp) = import_claude::load_import_arg(arg, cwd)?; handle.install_import(imp);
  behavior: sets recent (last TAIL_TURNS turns), files, last_reco; pending_snapshot = with_reco(reco, build_snapshot(import_note(stem))). Refused while busy or prompter waiting: UiEvent::Error("cannot import while a turn is running").
  test: engine::installed_import_snapshot_goes_in_front_of_next_prompt (PASS).
  doubt: Go importSession calls r.clear() before applyImport. Rust install does not reset the child or session id; the snapshot goes to the current child.
  doubt: restoreAgents (Go) not ported; live agent_info lines still added via build_snapshot.

- out of scope: cargo clippy -p chaude-core --tests -> FAIL in crates/core/src/record_turn.rs:261 (field assignment after Default, not my file). Acceptance clippy without --tests passes.

# MCP resident unix socket parity (run note)
status: done (local commit, no push)

branch: rust-port
scope: Go listenMCP parity; unix = unix socket 0600 in 0700 temp dir, Windows = TCP + token

## changed
- crates/core/src/mcp.rs: serve(run, token, sock) binds UnixListener on unix (no token line); TCP+token on Windows;
  Conn trait over TcpStream/UnixStream; start_owned makes 0700 dir, mcp.sock inside, guard removes dir on drop
- crates/cli/src/mcp_resident.rs: accepts unix socket path (tcp: still accepted); usage test updated
- crates/core/tests/parity_harness_p2.rs: serve() call updated; mcp_config_args_point_at_unix_socket un-ignored

## socket path
- unix: <temp_dir>/chaude-mcp-<pid>-<nanos>/mcp.sock (Go: MkdirTemp(os.TempDir()) + mcp.sock)
- mcp.json args: [mcp-resident, <socket path>]; env token still written (test expects it), unused on unix
- windows: tcp:127.0.0.1:<port>, token line required

# 261104 haiku resume_session + session log

status: done
commit: 6d035d1 feat(core): resume_session relaunches a session mid-run and logs session ids per record
scope: crates/core only (api.rs, handoff.rs, tests/engine.rs). mcp.rs and other modules untouched.

signature: pub fn EngineHandle::resume_session(&self, session_id: &str)
event: UiEvent::SessionResumed { old_session: String, session_id: String }
  - idle only; busy or prompter waiting -> UiEvent::Error("cannot resume while a turn is running")
  - empty id -> UiEvent::Error("resume needs a session id")
  - resume_mode does not apply: the caller names the session, so it always passes --resume
record log: <record folder>/.sessions.jsonl (handoff::SESSIONS_FILE)
  - handoff::append_session(exit_path, role, sid), handoff::read_sessions(exit_path) -> Vec<SessionRef>
  - SessionRef { session_id, role, saved_at_unix }; read dedups by session_id (first row wins)
  - written on main sid change (on_event System) and resident sid change (note_resident), only with handoff_path

checks:
  cargo fmt -p chaude-core -- --check -> PASS
  cargo build -p chaude-core -> PASS
  cargo test -p chaude-core -> PASS (89 lib + 52 engine, 0 failed)
  cargo clippy -p chaude-core -- -D warnings -> PASS
  cargo build -p chaude-cli -> PASS (SessionResumed does not break cli; UiEvent is non_exhaustive)

tests added:
  handoff::session_log_appends_and_reads_each_id_once
  engine::resume_session_relaunches_main_with_resume_flag (args.log has --resume old9; .sessions.jsonl has s1/main)
  engine::resume_session_rejects_empty_id

gaps:
  - resident sidecar write (note_resident) not covered by a test
  - busy rejection not covered by a test (race-prone with fake child)
  - no UiEvent for the sidecar; TUI must call handoff::read_sessions itself

task: tui event-driven redraw (crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs only)
status: done
design:
  - terminal reader thread and engine forwarder thread feed one mpsc<Wake> channel
  - loop blocks on wait_batch: recv() when idle, recv_timeout(TICK) only when App::animating()
  - animating = busy || copy_note.is_some(); idle = no timer, no polling
  - each wake batch: drain all queued wakes, apply, app.tick, mouse toggle, one draw
  - keypress in idle app wakes at once and draws in the same iteration
  - Resize still clears screen and prev; Quit/Closed end loop as before
fmt: cargo fmt -p chaude-cli applied; pre-existing diff in mod.rs fixed
tests:
  - a_key_press_wakes_the_loop_without_waiting_for_the_tick: key after 20 ms, 10 s timeout, returns <5 s with the key
  - idle_timeout_yields_an_empty_batch_and_closed_ends_the_wait
  - only_busy_or_a_copy_note_keeps_the_loop_on_a_timer
checks:
  - cargo build -p chaude-cli -> Finished
  - cargo test -p chaude-cli -> 167 passed, 0 failed

  - cargo fmt -p chaude-cli -- --check -> clean
gap:

  - no live terminal run performed

# 261104-haiku-tui-render-decouple
scope: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs only. crates/core not touched (mid-edit by other coder at time of run).
hot_spots:
- frame(): App::body() rewrapped the whole transcript (wrap_cells + wrap_linked) on every draw. 2200 rows -> ~11-13 ms/frame release.
- tui loop: one draw per wake batch, no frame cap. 200 events/s -> up to 200 full rewraps/s.
- engine handle calls (submit/interrupt/usage/...) are channel sends already; engine thread already off UI. no change needed.
- copy_text() runs pbcopy synchronously on mouse release (one-off, not per frame). not changed.
- editor.take() writes history file synchronously on submit (one-off per submit). not changed.
changes:
- WrapCache (RefCell in App): per transcript line keep (hash(text,width,linked), wrapped rows). body() rewraps only changed lines; frame reads cache.
- wrap_key / wrap_row helpers (same wrap logic as before).
- tui loop: dirty flag; frame drawn at most every FRAME=16ms (about 60 fps); wait_batch timeout = time to next due frame while dirty; TICK still drives spinner when animating. Keys/output in a batch applied before the draw check.
- bench: tui::tests::bench_frame_large_history_streaming (#[ignore]); 2000-row history, 200 streamed text events, one frame each.
frame_ms (release, bench, avg of 200 frames):
- before: 11.376 / 13.300 (p95 11.68 / 22.0) [two runs]
- after: 0.275, 0.284, 0.310, 0.669(noisy run), 0.459 (final tree) [p95 0.29-1.5]
- loop rate under 200 events/s: <=62.5 frames/s by construction. not measured end to end.
check:
- cargo test -p chaude-cli (in clean HEAD snapshot + this mod.rs, because working-tree core did not compile mid-edit) -> 204 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -> 0 warnings
- cargo fmt -p chaude-cli -- --check -> clean
scroll: no change to scroll state logic; existing scroll/typing/new-output tests pass.
doubts:
- working-tree crates/core failed to compile during this run (other coder). verified via snapshot of HEAD core.
- end-to-end loop rate and key latency (<=16ms wait while dirty) not measured in a real TTY.

## follow-up: changed parts only (user correction)
user: "아니 한번에 그리지 말고, 바뀌는 부분만 바꿔" (repaint only what changed).
still_full_redraw_before:
- body shift on streamed line: follow-tail moves every body row up by 1, so all 36 body rows were repainted (ESC[2K + row text).
- draw() itself already diffed rows; no terminal.clear() on normal updates. Resize still does Clear(All) (expected).
changes (crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs):
- layout(app,w,h) -> (rows, body_h); frame() is now #[cfg(test)] wrapper.
- body_shift(prev, rows, body_h): detects body moved up/down by 1..8 rows (k=0 first).
- draw(): when shifted, DECSTBM to body rows (ESC[1;{body_h}r), ScrollUp/ScrollDown(k), reset ESC[r; model prev rows as moved (scrolled-in rows blank), then diff as before. Chrome/input/status rows still diffed normally.
- test draw_scroll_region_leaves_the_screen_equal_to_the_frame: small VT model (CSI H/K2/S/T/r/m/h/l) fed draw() output, asserts screen == frame after each update incl. scroll -3,-2,5,100,-40, tail append, 20-line burst (k>8 full path). Also asserts a streamed line emits ESC[1S.
- bench: per update counts bytes and repainted rows (ESC[2K count in output) and cells = repainted rows * cols.
measure (release bench, history 2000 + 200 streamed = 2200 rows, 120x40, one streamed line per update, n=200):
- before (bytes avg/max): 3080 / 5985 (measured as logical diff; old draw repainted the same 36 rows)
- before (rows repainted avg/max): 36 / 36
- before (cells repainted avg/max): 4320 / 4320 (36 rows * 120 cols)
- after (bytes avg/max): 104 / 128
- after (rows repainted avg/max): 1 / 1
- after (cells repainted avg/max): 120 / 120
- frame ms after: avg 0.278 p50 0.278 p95 0.288 (unchanged from prior run, draw cost is not the bottleneck now).
check:
- cargo fmt -p chaude-cli (applied to mod.rs only)
- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> 205 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -> 0 warnings
- cargo test -p chaude-cli --release bench_frame -- --ignored --nocapture -> BENCH lines above
still_full_redraw_after:
- scroll moves larger than 8 rows, resize (Clear All), and overlays that change content (usage/sessions list) repaint changed rows only, but there is no scroll shortcut beyond 8 rows.
- not measured: busy spinner ticks (expected 1 status row per tick), real TTY/JediTerm support for DECSTBM + SU/SD (VT100 standard, not verified in a real terminal).
core change needed: none. crates/core not touched.

# 261105 haiku tui-cursor-ime-hide-toggle
status: done

## Checks
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> PASS (exit 0)
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings -> PASS (exit 0)
- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS (263 lib passed, 1 ignored; 4 bin passed)
- cargo build -p chaude-cli -> PASS; target/debug/chaude rebuilt 18:43

## Clipping review (draw / cursor / input_view_row)
- draw() clears and rewrites a whole changed row (Clear(CurrentLine) + row text). No partial erase of the cell before the cursor. Input row is in chrome, not the scroll-region body, so body_shift does not touch it.
- Reverse cell is at the display column of the cursor char (split_cells); wide char is reverse-drawn as 2 cells. No reverse cell at col-1 in normal flow.
- Park x = 3 + cur_col + ime_shift; with shift -2 the park lands on the first cell of the previous wide char, so a JediTerm preedit anchored at cursorX+1 lands on its second cell. This matches the reported half-cover. Expected from the shift value, not a draw bug.
- Shared with Go (not fixed): cursor at end of a row filled exactly to width appends a reverse space one column past inner (input.go View / input_view_row). Rust side() does not clip; Go same.
- Not verified at runtime in JetBrains.

## Test commands (binary: /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude)
- hide=0 shift=-1: CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=0 CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-1 /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude
- hide=0 shift=-2: CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=0 CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-2 /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude
- hide=1 shift=-1: CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=1 CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-1 /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude
- hide=1 shift=-2: CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=1 CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-2 /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude

# 261105 haiku tui-cursor-ime-park-audit
status: no edit (cause not proven from code; hypotheses + trace below)
workstream: tui-cursor, prior a95c2b5 (hide hardware cursor under JediTerm)
repo: /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness, branch rust-port

## Parity check (Go truth vs Rust)
- formula: Go tui.go:1018 curCol = 1+2+col+1+imeShift (1-based). Rust mod.rs:2246-2249 x = 1+pw+cur_col+ime_shift (0-based). Same cell.
- col units: Go input.go:173-181 Cursor() uses cells(). Rust input_rows mod.rs:1830-1860 uses UnicodeWidthChar. Both cell-based, Hangul = 2 cells in both.
- shift -1 for JetBrains-JediTerm: Go tui.go:177-188, Rust mod.rs:2271-2281. Same. Only TERMINAL_EMULATOR env gates it in both.
- park timing: Go cursorWriter tui.go:223-260 parks after EVERY write (moves down by upWas before next write). Rust parks only at end of draw() mod.rs:2302-2325, draw runs on dirty+FRAME or TICK (mod.rs:2700-2790).
- hide: bubbletea v1.3.4 standard_renderer.go:407 hideCursor, tea.go:443. Same as a95c2b5.
- overlay divergence: Go tui.go:1009 curCol=0 when perm != nil or !ready (no park). Rust cursor() always parks, even during permission prompt / overlays.

## Hypotheses (unverified, JediTerm runtime not available here)
H1: JediTerm preedit anchor is not exactly cursorX+1 in all states (Go comment cites TerminalPanel.drawInputMethodUncommitedChars, not re-checked). If anchor differs, preedit lands off by one.
H2: Hidden cursor (DECTCEM off, from a95c2b5) changes where JediTerm anchors preedit. Then "jump" could come from a95c2b5, not from the shift. Test: compare pre-a95c2b5 binary vs current under JediTerm.
H3: Parking under overlays (perm prompt, /view, /usage) where Go does not park. Would show preedit at input row while overlay is up. Symptom mismatch with report, low prior.
H4: TICK/FRAME redraws while busy re-emit MoveTo(x,y) each frame. Same coordinates, so not a jump source by itself.

## Checks run

# 261105 haiku tui-cursor-park-parity
status: partial (parity fix done; composing +1 cell symptom NOT reproduced or proven here)
workstream: tui-cursor, follows 261105-haiku-tui-cursor-ime-park-audit.md and 261105-tui-cursor-jediterm-double-cursor.md
repo: /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness, branch rust-port

## Done
- (1) park after every write: already equivalent. draw() ends with MoveTo(x,y) on every call (mod.rs ~2335).
  Other stdout writers do not move the cursor: copy_text OSC52 (mod.rs:2050), MouseCellMotion (mod.rs:2434), Clear(All) on resize (mod.rs ~2767). No change.
- (1b) overlay park: Go tui.go:1009 curCol=0 when perm != nil. Rust draw() now skips MoveTo when app.perm.is_some(). commit 78fe256.
  Go also checks !ready; Rust has no ready flag (size always set at start). Not ported.
- (2) shift math vs Go: identical.
  Go tui.go:1018 curCol = max(1, 1+2+col+1+imeShift) 1-based. Rust mod.rs cursor() x = 1+pw+cur_col+ime_shift 0-based, pw = width("> ") = 2. Same cell.
  Empty input: both give text-start cell (0-based 3) plus shift.
  Wide Hangul: Go cells() and Rust UnicodeWidthChar both count 2 cells. Reverse cell drawn at split_cells(row, cur_col) (mod.rs ~1949), same column.
- CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT: Go imeColShift tui.go:177, Rust ime_col_shift mod.rs ~2272. Both: trim, parse int, fallback to TERMINAL_EMULATOR=JetBrains-JediTerm -> -1, else 0. Values add to the 0-based parked column.

## Cause analysis (unproven)
- Shift -1 under JediTerm should land preedit on the drawn cell if JediTerm anchors at cursorX+1 (Go comment, not rechecked against JediTerm source).
- Reported +1 offset fits either:
  H-A: TERMINAL_EMULATOR not visible to chaude (shift 0 applied), so preedit at cursorX+1. Fix: CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-1.
  H-B: shift -1 applied, JediTerm anchor is cursorX+2 while the cursor is hidden. Fix: CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-2.
- Cannot tell A from B without the JetBrains runtime.

## Checks
- cargo test -p chaude-cli --lib draw_ -> PASS (9 passed, incl. new draw_does_not_park_the_cursor_under_a_permission_prompt)
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> PASS (only my new assert was off-format; applied rustfmt)

- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS (262 lib, 4 bin, exit 0)
- cargo build -p chaude-cli -> PASS; target/debug/chaude rebuilt 2026-11-05 18:38
- New test not run against pre-fix code (no revert check).

   - JetBrains-JediTerm: run CHAUDE_IME_SHIFT=-2 /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness/target/debug/chaude

# 261105 haiku tui-cursor-steady-blink
status: done
workstream: tui-cursor, follows 261105-haiku-tui-cursor-ime-hide-toggle.md
symptom: JetBrains user, Hangul correct with CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=0 or 1 (same result); cursor keeps blinking.
cause: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs (TermGuard::enter / restore_terminal) never set a cursor shape, so the terminal default (blinking block) applies to the shown/parked cursor. CHAUDE_IME_HIDE does not change the shape, which fits "same result" for both values.
fix: CursorShape(bool) Command (DECSCUSR CSI Ps SP q): steady block (Ps 2) on enter, terminal default (Ps 0) on restore_terminal.
changed: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs (CursorShape struct, enter + restore lists, test cursor_shape_is_steady_on_start_and_default_on_exit)
not changed: per-frame MoveTo/Show/Hide in draw() (mod.rs ~2346-2355). Skipping redundant cursor writes needs state across frames; other writers (release_copy OSC52, MouseCellMotion toggle) move the real cursor, so a stateful skip could leave IME parking wrong. Not done.
other per-frame: draw() runs every TICK (80ms) while busy (mod.rs ~2793-2802), re-emitting Show/Hide + park. Input row itself is rewritten only when its text changes.
reverse cell: render.rs:1068 input_view_row uses SGR 7 only; no SGR 5 blink.

## Go (deprecated/src/main/intt/internal/harness/tui.go)
- No HideCursor/ShowCursor/DECSCUSR anywhere in the harness. Real cursor stays at terminal default shape and is parked per frame by cursorWriter (tui.go:223-250, up N + column C after each Write).
- bubbletea v1.3.4 (/Users/leekwangseok/go/pkg/mod/github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea@v1.3.4): hideCursor only on HideCursor() msg (tea.go:442, screen.go:101); harness never sends it. Correction: 261105-tui-cursor-jediterm-double-cursor.md said bubbletea hides the cursor; that is not what v1.3.4 does for this program.
- bubbles v0.20.0 textinput blinks its cursor, but the harness input is custom (input.go), no bubbles cursor.
So Go shows the terminal-default (blinking) cursor too; Rust now diverges by steady block.

## Checks
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> PASS (exit 0)
- cargo test -p chaude-cli cursor_shape_is_steady -> PASS (1 passed)
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings -> PASS (no warnings)
- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS (265 lib passed, 1 ignored; 4 bin passed)
- cargo build -p chaude-cli -> PASS; target/debug/chaude rebuilt

## Doubts
- Not verified in JetBrains. JediTerm DECSCUSR support assumed. If blinking persists, next suspect is the per-frame Show/Hide (draw tail) resetting JediTerm's blink timer.
- Hide=1 path still hides; blink with Hide=1 would mean JediTerm draws its cursor regardless of DECTCEM.

## User verify
1. cd /Users/leekwangseok/PycharmProjects/claude-harness && CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=0 ./target/debug/chaude (JetBrains terminal)
2. Cursor should be a steady block in the input box while the TUI runs; after quit, the shell cursor returns to its normal shape.
3. Repeat with CHAUDE_IME_HIDE=1; report whether blinking remains.

# tui-view-crash: /view crash (panic in draw)

status: fixed, not committed
cause: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs:2406 body_shift `prev[..body_h]` panics (range end 8 > len 7) when overlay frame length != body_h
mechanism: layout() (mod.rs ~2343) used overlay_lines() result as-is; sessview list_lines() returns 1+min(n,5) rows (0 when width<10), not body_h.
  frame height drops below h; draw() second call compares prev/rows of equal short length, then slices prev[..body_h] -> panic.
  prompt_lines() already resize(hh), so only the list path broke.
repro: new test view_overlay_fills_the_body_and_redraws_at_any_size (mod.rs ~5383)
  - before fix: assert fails at 10x6 (frame rows 4 != 6); with height assert disabled, panic mod.rs:2406 "range end index 8 out of range for slice of length 7"
  - after fix: PASS
fix: mod.rs layout(): overlay Vec resized to body_h (truncate/pad with empty rows)
changed: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs (layout overlay normalize; new test)
checks:
  cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS (lib 269 passed, 1 ignored; bin 4 passed)
  cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -> PASS (no warnings shown)
  cargo fmt -p chaude-cli --check -> PASS (exit 0)
doubts:
  - list rows beyond body_h are now cut off; when body_h < 6 the selected row may be hidden (no panic, UX only)
  - Korean long text in list/prompt paths: no panic found (wrap_cells/clip/csi_len char boundaries checked)
  - not reproduced on real TTY; repro is draw() via unit test

cause: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs draw() emitted Show (?25h) every frame; Go bubbletea hides the hardware cursor (bubbletea tea.go:443) and only parks it for IME. Under JediTerm the shown hardware cursor sits one cell left (ime_col_shift -1) next to the drawn reverse-video cell.

# 261106 haiku drag autoscroll (tui-drag-scroll)
status: done
branch: rust-port (uncommitted, no commit made)
changed:
  - crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs (EdgeScroll, edge_delta, EDGE_TICK, App.edge, select_press/select_drag/select_release, edge_tick, edge_wait, loop timeout + edge_tick call, 6 unit tests)
  - CHANGELOG.md (Unreleased, English line only)
go_parity: none. v0.11.2 deprecated/src/main/intt/internal/harness/selection.go selectMouse has no edge scroll; drag head is only clamped into the viewport (Y clamp). Rust keeps that clamp and adds edge scroll on top.
behavior:
  - top row (y == 0): scroll up 1 row per 50 ms step
  - bottom row (y == body_h-1): scroll down 1 row per step
  - below body (y >= body_h): scroll down 3 rows per step (WHEEL_LINES)
  - stop: content top (scrolling up, start == 0), tail (scrolling down, follow set), pointer back inside, release, next press
  - head re-set to the edge line each step (selection extends to newly visible lines)
  - repeat drags on same edge keep the step schedule (no speed-up from movement events)
  - loop wait timeout = min(normal timeout, time to next edge step)
tests:
  - cargo test -p chaude-cli --lib -> 286 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored (includes scroll_bottom_on_input_and_update)
  - new: edge_delta_scrolls_on_the_edge_rows_and_three_rows_beyond, drag_on_the_top_row_scrolls_up_each_tick_and_extends_the_selection, drag_below_the_body_scrolls_down_three_rows_and_stops_at_the_tail, drag_on_the_bottom_row_scrolls_one_row_and_stops_at_the_top, moving_back_inside_or_releasing_stops_the_edge_scroll, repeated_drags_on_the_same_edge_keep_the_step_schedule
  - cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings -> exit 0
  - cargo build --release -p chaude-cli -> exit 0
  - cargo fmt --check: 3 pre-existing hunks in mod.rs (~1497, ~3787, ~3824) and sessview.rs import order; not touched
scope:
  - /view session pane (sessview.rs) has no drag selection code; no change there.
doubts:
  - CHANGELOG Korean line not added (agent rule: only haiku-tui writes Korean). Ask haiku-tui to add the KO line under the Unreleased entry.
  - body_h (with slash menu) is used for edge detection and head, but scroll() uses rows - chrome_len (pre-existing mismatch). edge_tick mirrors scroll(), so the tail/top stop matches scroll(); the edge row may differ from the real body bottom while the slash menu is open.
  - Edge scroll is not manually tested in a real terminal; only unit-tested via select_drag/edge_tick with injected Instant.

## Task 3: SendMessage resume, agent not shown running (2.1.318 transcript check)
- status: fixed in core; cli unchanged
- version: user runs chaude 0.11.2 · Claude Code 2.1.318 (per coordinator). Transcript 232d1a9a: 158 lines version 2.1.318.
- cause: after SendMessage resume, core sets agents[tool]=now and emits AgentResumed (row running). The resumed run's
  task-notification names the SendMessage tool_use id (e.g. toolu_01Rbk3xf2..., toolu_019jJ1SX...), not the Agent id.
  on_task_notification (crates/core/src/api.rs ~2924) looked up only the Agent id, so the resumed agent never ended.
  Reproduced: engine test resumed_agent_ends_by_notice_naming_its_sendmessage_id failed (no AgentEnd for toolu_bg1).
- fix: api.rs: new field resume_ids (SendMessage id -> agent tool id), set on resume (~2713), applied in
  on_task_notification (~2931).
- 2.1.318 shapes seen in transcript:
  - resume (idle agent): {"success":true,"message":"Resuming agent <short>","resumedAgentId":"<full>","pin":{...}} — handled.
  - queued (agent busy): {"success":true,"message":"Message queued for delivery to <id> at its next tool round. If it finishes first, it is resumed with the message and notifies again...","pin":{...}} — no resumedAgentId, so no AgentResumed; agent is already running, so no change needed.
  - failed: {"success":false,"message":"Agent \"<id>\" could not be resumed: No transcript found ..."} — handled (not resumed).
  - Launch text in 2.1.318 still contains "Async agent launched" + "agentId:" marker.
- check:
  - cargo test -p chaude-core --test engine resumed_ -> 2 passed (new test + existing resumed_background_agent_is_active_again_until_task_notification)
  - cargo test -p chaude-core -> pass (all suites)
  - cargo test -p chaude-cli -> 272 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored
  - cargo clippy -p chaude-core -p chaude-cli --all-targets -> no warnings
  - cargo fmt --check -> rc 0
- changed: crates/core/src/api.rs; crates/core/tests/engine.rs (new test); crates/core/tests/fixtures/resume_notice_names_sendmessage.jsonl (new)
- not done in this session: task 1 (/view scrolling; code already has level-2 scroll, untested at App level) and task 2 (naming model-purpose(action); needs linkage design). Not changed.
- risk: the queued case (agent busy when SendMessage) is not covered by a test; the "notifies again" notice for a queued resume may name the SendMessage id too, which the alias now covers only if the SendMessage id was recorded as a resume.
- no commit, no push, no GitHub action.

## Task 1: issue #3 /view scrolling
- status: already implemented in SessView (level 2); gap was no App-level test. Test added, no behavior change.
- keys level 1 (list): Up/Down or k/j select, Enter open, Esc or q close.
- keys level 2 (prompt): Up/Down or k/j line scroll, PgUp/PgDn page, Home/End top/bottom, Left/Right prev/next prompt, Esc back to list.
- mouse wheel: 3 lines per notch (App::overlay_wheel -> SessView::wheel); clamped at both ends.
- test: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs view_prompt_scrolls_through_app_keys_and_wheel (on_key PageDown/End/Home/PageUp/Esc, overlay_wheel, header "-60/60").

## Task 2: names in /view list and window header
- format: "<model>-<purpose>(<first prompt, 40 chars>)"; model family = model after "claude-" up to first "-" (claude-opus-5-5 -> opus).
- purpose: "main" for the main session, resident role name (coder, prompter, tui, ...) from UiEvent::SessionIds.
- fallback: 8-char session id when purpose or transcript model is unknown (e.g. owned sessions from handoff, not in this run).
- example: "haiku-coder(Fix the build)"; main: "opus-main(<first prompt>)".
- changed: crates/cli/src/tui/sessview.rs (SessionInfo.model via parse_model; SessView.purposes; name_of; list_lines and prompt_lines header use name_of; match_sessions and format_session_list stay id-based); crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs (App.session_purpose filled in SessionIds handler; open_sessions sets sv.purposes).
- not done: subagents. /view lists session transcripts only; subagents have no session file and AgentRow is keyed by the Agent tool id, so "haiku-coder(<task description>)" needs new linkage. Not changed.
- tests: sessview session_names_are_model_purpose_and_fall_back_to_the_id; parse_model_reads_the_first_assistant_model; mod view_prompt_scrolls_through_app_keys_and_wheel.

## Checks (this session, after cargo fmt --all)
- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> 275 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored (lib); 4 passed (bin)
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -> no warnings or errors
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> rc 0

## Draft reply for issue #3 (not posted)
Thanks for the report. /view session windows now scroll: Up/Down (or k/j) move a line, PgUp/PgDn a page, Home/End jump to the top or bottom, and the mouse wheel moves 3 lines. The window stops at the first and last line. Session rows and window titles now show a name (for example `haiku-coder(Fix the build)`) instead of only the 8-character id. This will ship in the next release.

## Explanation for the resume fix (one line)
Before the fix the SendMessage-resumed agent row showed as running, but its completion notice named the SendMessage tool id, so core never ended it and the row stayed in the running list.

root_causes:
- SendMessage closed at its own ToolEnd (api.rs on_tool_result): ToolEnd was emitted before AgentResumed. Fixed: no ToolEnd for a resuming send; AgentResumed carries send_id; TUI moves the send row into resume_rows (mod.rs AgentResumed handler). Row closes at AgentEnd (handback or task-notification).
- running panel text was yellow (RUNNING_ON) in HEAD: Go is dim grey text with a 220 dot. Fixed in agent_row_line (mod.rs). Per-state dot colour map matches Go agentDot (verified by grep).
- top bar had no colour escape (chrome()); body rows could leave an SGR open and it leaked into the next row. Fixed: chrome() wraps top bar in GREY_ON/FG_OFF; draw() appends RESET after each row. Source body row that left SGR open was NOT identified; the reset makes it unnecessary.

  - cause: fake claude script appended every spawn's argv line to one shared role_args.log with `>>`. 4 spawns start at once, and a long main argv (~3KB system prompt) was split across write() calls, so another process's line landed mid-line. The tail fragment "des about the current work..." counted as a 5th role.
  - fix: fake writes `DIR/role_args.$$.log` (one file per process, `>` not `>>`). test reads all role_args.* files via closure role_args(dir).
  - verify: rustfmt/cargo fmt --check PASS; 10/10 solo runs of resident_slots_fixed_four PASS.
  - left uncommitted: crates/core/tests/engine.rs
- doubts:
  - 10/10 is weak evidence for a ~1/10 race. not re-run the pre-fix loop for a second confirmation.
  - main.pids and args.log in the fake still use `>>` with long argv. no test reads main.pids in this path; not changed.
  - CHANGELOG entries describe the Windows pid fix and the restored summary behavior; wording is mine, review if needed.

## fixed
- bug: agent pane wheel did not clamp. overlay_wheel called av.wheel(), which adds 3 to off with no bound; draw clamps only a clone. 50 wheel-up notches on a 20-line log left off=150 (max 13), so wheel-down looked dead for about 35 notches.
- fix: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs overlay_wheel: when av_shown(), call self.scroll(±WHEEL_LINES) (main pane clamp, reused). av.wheel() kept only for the hidden-pane case (rw open).
- test: agent_pane_wheel_stops_at_the_oldest_line_and_answers_at_once (mod.rs). Before fix: FAILED left Some(150) right Some(13). After fix: ok.
- fix 2 (coordinator follow-up): agentview.rs key() Up/k, Down/j, PageUp, PageDown, Home, End added to off with no bound. key() now returns Action::Scroll(delta) (new variant), and mod.rs app key path calls self.scroll(delta) when av_shown() (same clamped path as wheel and edge). Home = isize::MIN/2, End = isize::MAX/2 (scroll clamps to oldest line / tail). Page size unchanged (max(1, vp_h-2)).
- test 2: agent_pane_keys_stop_at_the_oldest_line_and_answer_at_once (mod.rs). Before fix: FAILED left Some(50) right Some(13). After fix: ok. Also agentview.rs scroll_keys_follow_go updated to assert Action::Scroll values.
- process note: a cargo fmt --crate run and an Edit dropped one #[test] attr in agentview.rs; restored, count 9 again. Final lib run confirms 300 tests (300 passed, 1 ignored).

# 261107 haiku agent pane (agent-status workstream)

status: partial
branch: rust-port (uncommitted)

## Goal 1 (done mark missing for finished agents)
cause: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs agent_done. The finished record (finished_agents) was inserted only when the id was in self.agents. An AgentEnd with no local start row got no done entry in the agent list, while the done note still printed.
fix: agent_done always inserts the done record; label = start desc, else AgentEnd name; started_at = start time or 0.
test: tui::tests::agent_end_without_local_start_lists_done (synthetic AgentEnd, no AgentStart). Failed before fix, passes after.
unproven: the real trigger on Windows and mac is not reproduced. Prior run's replace_file retry (agent_store.rs) remains a separate candidate.
Ruled out or not confirmed: SubagentStop ledger path, CRLF in proc.rs (earlier run).

## Goal 2 (agent pane must stay open after finish)
finding: no auto-close code path in TUI. self.av is set only at mod.rs:1821 (click) and mod.rs:946 (Ctrl+T), and cleared only at mod.rs:932 on AgentView::key Close (Esc/q/Ctrl+C).
risk: agent_recs fallback (mod.rs:1424-1431, unwrap_or_default) can render a blank pane if the id is missing from agent_recs. Not changed.
status: no fix, no test (nothing to regress).

## Goal 3 (resumed agent pane shows only the new turn)
finding: agent_logs (mod.rs:159, 725) is never cleared or reset on AgentResumed (mod.rs:468-500). In-process resume keeps the same tool id (api.rs:2730, 2771), so earlier turns stay in the log.
gap: the earlier turns are lost when the key changes. That happens after a TUI restart, where only the "(restored) summary" line is loaded (api.rs restore path ~2843-2855). Not reproduced with a core fixture.
status: no fix, no test. Needs a core replay fixture to confirm.

cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS (290 passed, 1 ignored; plus 4 bin tests)

- spec: finished agent hidden from TUI agent list; SendMessage resume reappears running, hidden again on finish; open pane stays open at finish; resume pane shows only new turn.
- changed: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs
- resume detection: mod.rs UiEvent::AgentResumed arm (~468), branch `if !self.agents.iter().any(...)`; resets finished_agents and agent_logs for id before push to agents.
- agent_recs(): finished_agents block removed (list = running + restored rows).
- view_rec(id): new; agent_recs() else finished_agents with agent_logs; overlay_lines av branch uses it.
- agent_done fallback (Goal 1) kept.

## follow-up 2: handback on every turn end + idle exit
- spec: turn end -> done + handback to main; idle timeout -> child exits (ClaudeProc::finish); SendMessage/task after exit respawns with --resume same session.
- handback audit:
  - Agent spawn/SendMessage resume: AgentEnd emitted by finish_agent (api.rs ~2937-2965) and abandon_turn (api.rs ~3148); main's reply is the native handback (task-notification). Covered by existing tests handback_launch_agentid_lets_sendmessage_resume_show_a_panel_row, idle_handback_reply_is_shown_with_residents_on. No new test added for these.
  - resident coder: finish_coder (api.rs ~2097) Err branch emitted only Stage Failed; now also emits UiEvent::CoderReply { text: "오류: <reason>" }. Gap closed.
- idle exit:
  - Config.agent_idle_exit_secs: u64, default 600, 0 = never (api.rs ~345, ~446).
  - agent_idle_exit_left (api.rs ~2013), exit_idle_coder (api.rs ~2024) uses ClaudeProc::finish(CODER_EXIT_WAIT=5s); session kept in resume_pending.
  - wired into wait_budget (api.rs ~3074) and on_quiet (api.rs ~3146). applies to all coder slots incl. base.
  - proc.rs / shutdown untouched.
- tests (crates/core/tests/engine.rs):
  - failed_coder_task_still_hands_back_a_reply (~2889): empty reply -> CoderReply "오류:" -> PASS (red before fix not run)
  - idle_resident_exits_then_resumes_the_same_session (~2913, agent_idle_exit_secs=1): child pid dead after idle; next task respawns with --resume c1 -> PASS

  - cargo test --workspace -> PASS (engine 109 passed, 0 failed)
- doubts:
  - Agent-tool subagents run inside main child; no separate process, so idle exit applies only to coder slots.
  - base coder spawned at start has idle_since None -> no idle exit until its first task.
  - SendMessage to a coder is not a path; respawn is via start_coder (task).
  - extra coders still expire at coder_idle_ttl 270s (earlier than 600s).
  - agent_idle_exit_secs not wired to CLI flag/settings; default only.
  - Windows behavior not run; kill -0 test uses unix kill.

# 261107 haiku resume-go-sessions

task: why Go-era chaude sessions missing from Rust /resume picker; fix + synthetic test
status: done
branch: rust-port (not committed, not pushed)

## cause
- root: GroupPicker::new truncated groups to MAX_ROWS=30 (crates/cli/src/tui/resume_pick.rs). No scroll past 30.
- measured (read-only scratch crate outside repo, path dep on chaude-core; removed after):
  - main project key transcripts: 245 sessions readable (list_sessions), 120 groups (group_sessions, no clear marks)
  - groups newest-first: #1-#30 all from day 2026-11-03..11-07; #31-#120 = Oct 28..Nov 2 (Go-era)
  - 90 of 120 groups fall beyond the cap, so Go-era groups never render with no terms
- Go-era transcripts parse fine: 0 bad JSON lines, user/assistant events present (checked 3 oldest files, key counts only)
- matching: with terms, match_groups filters before the cap, so `/resume <term>` can reach old groups

## ruled out
- project key encoding (per-char non-alnum to '-', same as Go nonAlnum)
- reader rejecting Go-era shape (read_claude_transcript returns turns for oldest files)
- list_sessions filters (jsonl only, turns>0)
- record folder naming (folder_name matches Go)
- clear-mark cwd bug (fixed in 0.11.6, different symptom)

## change
- crates/cli/src/tui/resume_pick.rs
  - removed MAX_ROWS const and truncate in GroupPicker::new (lines() already windows around sel)
  - added test groups_beyond_thirty_stay_reachable (45 synthetic groups; wheel up reaches oldest; enter picks it; row drawn)
- parity note: Go tui.go capped at 30. Rust now shows all matches, scrollable. Product call if the cap must stay.

## checks
- cargo test -p chaude-cli resume_pick -> PASS (4 passed)
- cargo clippy --all-targets -> PASS (no warnings, Finished)
- cargo test -> PASS (all suites ok; 107-test suite 102.7s)

## risk
- picker with 120 rows: wheel/1-9 behavior unchanged for top 9; deep rows reached by wheel only
- no ws id; no commit

task: session-save on quit (chaude-rust / session-save)
status: partial
cause: quit killed claude child immediately (SIGKILL-equivalent) without stdin EOF or grace, so child-side transcript writes may not flush.
  - crates/core/src/api.rs:1207-1219 (before fix) called p.kill() on proc/prompter/tui/coders at shutdown
  - crates/core/src/proc.rs:267-270 (before fix) kill() = child.kill() + wait()
  - unproven: whether claude flushes transcript on stdin EOF; Windows behavior not run locally
fix:
  - crates/core/src/proc.rs: ClaudeProc.stdin -> Option<ChildStdin>; new finish(wait): drop stdin, poll try_wait up to wait, then kill
  - crates/core/src/api.rs: shutdown uses finish(Duration::from_secs(3)) for proc, prompter, tui, coders (drains still kill)
test added:
  - crates/core/src/proc.rs: finish_lets_child_flush_on_stdin_eof (unix only; sh fake child writes $1 after stdin EOF)
checks:
  - cargo test -p chaude-core --lib proc:: -> 10 passed
  - cargo fmt --all -- --check -> rc 0
  - cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings -> Finished, no warnings
  - cargo clippy -p chaude-core --all-targets --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc -D warnings -> Finished (x86_64-pc-windows-gnu not installed; msvc used)
  - cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast -> 1 FAILED: crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs:5077 tui::tests::agent_end_without_local_start_lists_done (other agent's in-progress agent-pane work; mod.rs has 52 uncommitted lines not from this run); all other suites pass
not done (remaining):

status: done

cause:
  engine start mode hardcoded PermMode::Auto (core/src/api.rs spawn, State init).
  TUI bar.mode hardcoded "auto" in App::new (cli/src/tui/mod.rs). Both matched by coincidence.
  Engine start state is not announced (ModeChanged only on /mode), so bar never learned real start mode.

fix:
  single source = Config.perm_mode (default Auto). Engine State.mode seeded from it.
  TUI run() reads cfg.perm_mode before Engine::try_start and calls App::set_start_mode.
  Bar then follows ModeChanged as before.

changed:
  crates/core/src/api.rs: Config.perm_mode field (+Default Auto); spawn: let perm_mode = cfg.perm_mode; State.mode = perm_mode.
  crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs: App::set_start_mode(core PermMode); tui(): start_mode captured before engine start, applied after App::new; test status_bar_starts_on_the_engine_start_mode.
  crates/core/tests/engine.rs: test configured_start_mode_is_the_engine_mode (Config perm_mode Plan -> /mode reports plan).

checks:
  cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings -> PASS (exit 0)
  cargo test --workspace -> PASS (exit 0)
    cli lib unit: 288 passed, 1 ignored (includes status_bar_starts_on_the_engine_start_mode)
    core engine tests: 107 passed (includes configured_start_mode_is_the_engine_mode)
    all test binaries: 0 failed
  targeted: configured_start_mode_is_the_engine_mode ok; status_bar_starts_on_the_engine_start_mode ok
  cargo fmt --check -> FAIL only in crates/cli/src/tui/resume_pick.rs (not touched by this run; pre-existing or other workstream). My files clean.

doubts:
  1. plain() path (cli/src/tui/mod.rs ~line 3194) still builds Ctx with PermMode::Default, so bare /mode in plain line mode reports "default" even when engine starts elsewhere. Not in scope for status bar; fix would track ModeChanged in plain loop.
  2. Two PermMode enums: core/src/mode.rs and cli/src/tui/commands.rs (duplicate, string-mapped). Not unified; bar uses as_str strings so they agree.
  3. No CLI flag sets perm_mode yet; only Config (tests/embedding). Default unchanged (auto), so runtime behavior unchanged today.
  4. proc.rs PERMISSION_MODE "auto" is the claude child's --permission-mode flag, separate from harness mode; left as is.
  5. resume_pick.rs shows as modified in git status; not edited by this run.

# 261107 haiku-win-agent-done

task: Windows finished agent intermittently lacks done mark (TUI agent list)
branch: rust-port (no commit, push, reset, stash)

## cause (hypothesis, not proven on Windows)
- done path: finish_agent (crates/core/src/api.rs:2933-2962) -> persist_agent(STO_DONE) -> AgentStore::put -> flush
- flush (crates/core/src/agent_store.rs ~306-308 before fix): tmp write then fs::rename(tmp, path); on rename failure the result is silently dropped and no retry happens until another change
- Windows: rename over a file another process has open (concurrent merge_disk fs::read) fails transiently (sharing violation); intermittent, matches symptom
- live TUI done mark comes from UiEvent::AgentEnd (crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs:518-530), not from the file; the file loss shows as interrupted/running after restart or resume
- ruled out: ledger.py SubagentStop hook (writes record/LEDGER.md only); hook/transcript markers not used by Rust done path; CRLF (proc.rs lines()/trim); child_set canonicalize (not on done path)

## change
- crates/core/src/agent_store.rs: flush uses new fn replace_file (retries fs::rename up to RENAME_RETRIES=10 with RENAME_RETRY_WAIT=20ms); success path on unix is one rename call (unchanged)
- added `use std::thread;`, consts RENAME_RETRIES, RENAME_RETRY_WAIT

## test
- chaude-core lib agent_store::tests::agent_store_replace_retries_busy_target
  (directory at target stands in for busy file, released after 50ms; asserts rename succeeds and content matches)
- note: a real Windows sharing violation cannot be reproduced on macOS; the test covers the retry contract only

## checks
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> exit 0
- cargo test -p chaude-core --lib agent_store -> 6 passed, 0 failed
- cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings -> exit 0
- cargo clippy -p chaude-core --all-targets --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc -- -D warnings -> exit 0
- cargo test --workspace -> all result lines ok, 0 failed (largest: 107 passed in 109.54s)
- x86_64-pc-windows-gnu not installed; msvc target used for clippy only (no link/run)

## unproven
- real Windows: whether the failing rename is the actual cause; whether 200ms retry budget is enough
- no Windows test run performed

## risk
- retries block the store mutex up to ~200ms only on failure; success path unchanged on Mac/Linux

# 261109 haiku-coder idle 3570 / 270

status: done
workstream: chaude-rust / main-idle-1h (follow-up to 261108 idle-timeouts)
rule: regular agents = 270 s; everything else = 3570 s (main, base coder, extra coders, WS_MAX_IDLE, --idle-rotate default). prompter/tui: no idle exit, none added.

## changed
- crates/core/src/api.rs: Config defaults idle_rotate 3570, coder_idle_ttl 3570, base_idle_exit_secs 3570, agent_idle_exit_secs 270; field comments.
- crates/core/src/api.rs: agent_idle_exit_left applies only to base coder (i==0); extra coders expire via coder_idle_left (coder_idle_ttl) only.
- crates/core/src/api.rs: startup reconcile deadline = cfg.idle_rotate (was cache window 290 s).
- crates/core/src/api.rs: tests idle_deadline_matches_go_cases, main_idle_rotation test use 3570.
- crates/core/src/clients.rs: test fixtures 3599 -> 3570 (idle_deadline cache-window 290 unchanged).
- crates/core/src/orch.rs: WS_MAX_IDLE 3570; RULE_F text; comments; tests at 317/319/398 (+3570/+3571/+3600).
- crates/cli/src/repl_args.rs: --idle-rotate default 3570; test 322. Explicit --idle-rotate=5m test unchanged.
- crates/cli/src/tui/settings.rs: comment line 33 and Korean label line 253 (3570초).
- crates/core/tests/parity_harness_p3.rs: defaults test (coder_idle_ttl 3570); idle_defaults test renamed and set to 3570/3570/3570/270.
- notes/DECISIONS.md: line 58 [idle] rewritten to the user rule.
- docs/guide/cli.md:119 (--idle-rotate 3570s), docs/guide/repl.md:186, docs/guide/models-and-agents.md:100 (extra coders 3570s).

## verdicts
- startup reconcile (api.rs ~1140): CAN retire. reconcile() (clients.rs ~306-334) sets EXPIRED instead of PARKED for LIVE rows whose owner died and whose last_api is older than the deadline. Old deadline 290 s retired main/base/extra rows. Fixed to idle_rotate (3570 s). Runs only at startup, never kills a live child.
- pick_child warm check (api.rs ~1435, pick_child.rs ~58-75): does NOT idle-kill. It chooses UseLive / Resume / StartNew at a switch request. A cold (>290 s, 5m) live child of the same model is replaced by StartNew with a handoff turn. Kept on the 290 s cache window: cache-cost choice only. Doubt: this can end a main child before 3570 s on a switch request under observed 5m cache.
- estimate_next (api.rs ~2264-2270): cache-warmth cost estimate, not rotation. Left at 290/3600.

## leftovers (grep 3599|290|4:50|59:59)
- notes/DECISIONS.md:21, :23: historical 261028 rules. Kept.
- plan/interactive-child-resident-sessions.md:18,48,96: Go-port plan, historical. Kept.

- crates/core/src/api.rs:2264,2270 (estimate_next), 3471, 4546, 4703-4704 (warm_deadline test): cache window. Kept.

- docs/pingpong-bench.md:29: token count 290255. Unrelated.

## hypotheses (ranked)
- H1 (most supported): the drained child's stdout does not echo an Agent-tool (SubagentHandback/task-notification) completion as a user line. drain_completed has nothing to match, so the agent stays running until drain_timeout (default 3600 s) or until reap_drains sees agents.len()==0. - non-rotated (repl): same prompt. Pane 21:07:24 same premature "[agent 완료]". stdout.raw line 159 task_notification completed (toolu_015aZ...) at 21:10:42; main replied DONE-ROT at pane 21:10:59. PASS for main turn and report text. TUI `[agent done]` text absent in both panes (0 matches).
- Cause (evidence, not yet fixed): `idle_result` -> `finish_oldest_background` ends the oldest background agent when a subagent-owned text result (parent_task_id set) arrives while main is idle. Agent row goes done at 21:07:25, before sleep ends. Rotation then sees agents empty, closes old child (no drain), and the owned sleep is killed.
- Not done: resumed-agent test, rotated-main reply time check beyond the above, hash of v0.11.10 asset (see below).

## 21:2x blocker (no code edit this round)
status: blocked
done: removal of finish_oldest_background cannot be applied safely yet. No api.rs/engine.rs edit made in this round.
evidence:
- captures have 0 text-form `<task-notification>` user lines (grep over norot/rot stdout.raw). Completion signals are all system/task_notification with tool_use_id.
- norot: line 159 system task_notification toolu_015aZ (completed) then main-level text turn with Result (line ~179 is the usage process; the reply turn is 160-178). That idle Result calls idle_result → finish_oldest_background, which is what ends the row in norot. Main path ignores system lines (on_event System handles session/model only). Removing the heuristic without a main-path system handler leaves norot row running.
- rot: line 134 system task_notification toolu_01SHr (Agent tool, parent of subagent text 130-132) at 21:07:22, BEFORE rotation (21:08:33) and before the sleep ends (b3g0j5j0r, toolu_015mK, ended 21:08:38, line 301).
conflict: a main-path system handler by tool_use_id finishes toolu_01SHr at 21:07:22, so rotation sees agents=0 and kills the sleep (same bug). Not handling it breaks norot row. The directive's test order (notification after rotation) does not match the capture order (134 before rotation).
hypotheses:
- H-A: the Agent-level task_notification (134) is the agent's completion; the sleep is a separate Bash task and must also be tracked (agent should stay running until its own Bash child ends: toolu_015mK) — needs coordinator decision.
- H-B: finish on main by system/task_notification only when no tool-level child of that agent is still running; needs the Bash child's id mapping.
decision needed: which id ends the agent row (134 Agent-level at 21:07:22, or 301 sleep at 21:08:38), and whether the sleep counts as agent work for rotation.

  residual risk: if the abandoned child never emits its result, the next turn's result is swallowed (stray=1) and that turn stays busy until a later result. No test added; not fixed here (brief: no redesign).

doubts:
  - engine full run had 1 ignored test (pre-existing attribute, not checked here).
  - git diff --stat of api.rs/engine.rs is cumulative from prior uncommitted coder work, not this round.

task: output pane link/path color leaks into plain text when a selection spans link + plain (chaude TUI)
status: done (no commit, no push, no release, no install)
root_cause:
  - render.rs highlight_line(): format!("{pre}\x1b[7m{strip(mid)}\x1b[27m{post}") removes every SGR inside the selected cells (mid), including the link-off escape (39m)
  - selection starting inside a link: link-on stays in pre, so selected cells keep link fg (blue reversed) and cells after the selection stay blue (the 39m was stripped)
  - selection covering a link's head: link-on sits in mid and is stripped, so the rest of the link loses its color
  - selection-only path: restyle output (after selection clears) was already correct; the bug is only in highlight_line
changed:
  - crates/cli/src/tui/render.rs (highlight_line; new helpers csi_escapes, link_fg_open; new test selection_across_link_keeps_link_color_on_link_cells_only)
fix:
  - selected cells drop a link fg open at the selection start (FG_OFF inside reverse only; other fg colors untouched)
  - SGR inside the selected cells is replayed after \x1b[27m, so the state after the selection is exact
  - reverse-video highlight itself unchanged
checks:
  - repro first: new test FAILED on old code: "see /tmp/foo and more text" 2..9 -> link "foo" lost color; 6..14 -> selected plain cells still link-colored
  - cargo test -p chaude-cli selection_across_link -> 1 passed
  - cargo test -p chaude-cli -> lib 324 passed, 1 ignored, 0 failed; bin 4 passed, 0 failed
  - cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings -> rc 0
  - rustfmt --edition 2024 on render.rs only (edition 2024 from Cargo.toml); --check rc 0; HEAD version was already clean
cases in test:
  - "see /tmp/foo and more text" sel 2..9, 6..14, 13..20, 0..26
  - "open https://x.io/p and more" sel 5..20
  - after clearing selection: link runs == link text only
doubts:
  - URL underline (4m from LINK_URL_ON) stays active in the selected cells when selection starts inside a URL; test checks color only
  - the other coder's edits (README.md, install.sh, install.ps1) untouched and not in this change

# 261110 haiku-coder restore rows

status: partial (engine suite not verified; it hangs in this environment)
task: restored agent rows showed "중단 · 모델 X" after reopen

## root cause
- core `restored_row` (api.rs:890) kept only done=true; no failed/interrupted flag from the saved status.
- CLI `agent_row_line` (cli/src/tui/mod.rs ~2597) and `agent_recs` (mod.rs ~1400) treated every done row as Interrupted.
- Restored done rows got the current session model as fallback, so a finished agent showed the wrong model.
- Agent launch without a `model` input saved an empty model; restored rows had no real model to show.
- `bar::agent_status_text` rendered an empty model as a bare "-" segment.

## changes
- crates/core/src/api.rs
  - AgentRow: new `failed`, `interrupted` fields.
  - restored_row: failed = status==failed; interrupted = status==interrupted||running; done=true; model kept.
  - AgentStart input.model stays as typed (live row unchanged, engine test expects "").
  - persist_agent: saved model = info.model, or the session model when the input had none.
  - import STO_FAILED; new test restored_row_keeps_saved_status_and_model.
- crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs
  - agent_row_line: state from failed/interrupted/done; session-model fallback only for live rows.
  - agent_recs: failed/interrupted from the row instead of hardcoded.
  - new tests: restored_done_row_shows_done_with_saved_model, restored_interrupted_row_keeps_saved_model,
    restored_failed_row_shows_failed, restored_row_without_model_omits_model_segment.
  - restored_agents_* and restored_row_opens_* tests set interrupted: true; running_panel_dot test likewise.
  - removed needless `..Default::default()` in AgentRec literal (clippy -D warnings).
- crates/cli/src/tui/bar.rs
  - agent_status_text: empty model -> omit model segment.
  - assert added in agent_status_text_uses_state_model_and_name.

## checks
- cargo test -p chaude-core --lib -> PASS, 269 passed, 0 failed
- cargo test -p chaude-cli -> PASS, 310 passed, 1 ignored (lib); 4 passed (bin)
- cargo clippy -p chaude-core --no-deps -- -D warnings -> PASS (exit 0)
- cargo clippy -p chaude-cli --no-deps -- -D warnings -> PASS (exit 0)
- rustfmt --edition 2024 --check on api.rs, mod.rs, bar.rs -> PASS
- cargo test -p chaude-core --test engine -> NOT PASSED. Run hangs.
  - threaded run: agent_rows_carry_panel_fields_for_a_background_launch failed once before the persist fix; the later run passed it.
  - single-thread run (--test-threads=1): stuck at drained_agent_done_summary_is_handback_message for >3 min (engine.rs:1097, loop on recv_timeout until Rotated).
  - hang cause not confirmed; not isolated to my edits.
  - isolated run: cargo test -p chaude-core --test engine drained_agent_done_summary_is_handback_message -- --exact
    -> FAILED, 0 passed 1 failed, finished in 61.34s; panic engine.rs:817 "agent never ended" (until_agent_end WAIT expired).
  - that test covers the rotation drain path (rotation preload work, engine.rs modified by another agent), not restored_row/persist_agent.
  - no fix attempted for it (out of scope); baseline vs HEAD not checked (stash/reset forbidden).

## doubts
- Saved model for a no-input agent is read at persist time (self.model), so a /model switch before the final save changes it.
- Startup restore still uses AgentStore::load("") (interrupted only); done rows appear only via paths that include them. Kept for Go-parity test agent_restore_marks_interrupted.
- Engine suite not green; the acceptance item is open.
- api.rs and mod.rs changed on disk during this run (another agent). Edits applied cleanly; re-check diff before merge.
- No AgentStart-level test for the model fallback.
- No commit, push, or release.

# 261110 haiku-coder windows localtime

task: Windows HH:MM:SS stamps showed UTC instead of local (KST). Use one shared local-time helper.
status: done (no commit/push/release/install)

cause:
- crates/cli/src/tui/mod.rs clock_hms: #[cfg(not(unix))] branch used UTC (rem_euclid 86400). Feeds clock_hhmm/clock_hhmmss.
- crates/cli/src/tui/sessview.rs local_parts: #[cfg(not(unix))] branch used utc_parts. Feeds session list stamp.
- crates/core/src/api.rs local_stamp (record file name): shelled out to `date`; no `date` exe on Windows, so Windows returned None and the turn was not saved.

  unix: libc::localtime_r -> offset. windows: SystemTimeToTzSpecificLocalTime (kernel32, per-date DST, no new crate). other: 0.
- crates/core/Cargo.toml: [target.'cfg(unix)'.dependencies] libc = "0.2" (already in Cargo.lock).
- crates/core/src/lib.rs: pub mod localtime.
- mod.rs clock_hms, sessview.rs local_parts, api.rs local_stamp now call the shared helper. utc_parts removed; its test replaced.
- mac/linux output: same local time as before (localtime_r path kept).

changed:

# 261111 haiku coder agent-queue-core: caveats (a) no hold, (b) one final reply, (c) early report leak

key: workstream: agent-queue-core (continues 261111-haiku-coder-agent-queue-core-wait.md)
key: changed paths: crates/core/src/api.rs (uncommitted; this session's edits: late guard in complete_agent, held_turn + note_held_turn, release_held deferral, end_turn drop-while-held, cancel_wait clears held_turn)
key: new tests: api::tests agent_queue_settled_before_result_is_not_held, agent_queue_early_report_while_held_does_not_leak_or_double_reply, agent_queue_late_report_after_timeout_is_not_a_second_reply
key: test harness: agent_queue_engine_with(..., extra: Option<(&str,&str)>, inline: &str); agent_queue_engine delegates with None, ""
key: no commit; no other paths touched

## causes
- (a) run1 no hold: timing, not wiring. Both agents finished before main's Result, so pending was 0 and no hold is correct. Covered by agent_queue_settled_before_result_is_not_held (characterization).
- (b) two causes: (1) late task_notification after timeout started a notice turn -> second reply (complete_agent late guard); (2) release_held could write the continuation into a child turn still streaming (held_turn deferral in release_held/end_turn).
- (c) early hand-back while held arrived as a child turn; its Result went through finish_turn -> start_tui and was shown. Fix: end_turn drops that turn's text while held, no TUI start; cancel_wait restores held text as the reply.
- assertion basis: TuiAnswer text is the tui resident's answer ("ok" in fixture), not the main text. Tests assert ResidentBusy{role:"tui", busy:true, prompt} list instead: ["waitingfirst final"] (settled), ["all done"] (early, late).

## checks
- cargo test -p chaude-core agent_queue -> 8 passed, 0 failed (log: notes/tmp/test/agent-queue.log)
- cargo fmt --all -- --check -> exit 0 (after cargo fmt --all; only api.rs was flagged, 4 hunks in new tests)
- cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings -> exit 0 (log: notes/tmp/test/clippy.log)
- cargo test --workspace -> exit 0; sum of "test result" lines: passed 905, failed 0 (log: notes/tmp/test/ws-test.log)
- cargo build --release -p chaude-cli -> exit 0 (log: notes/tmp/test/build.log)
- rm -rf target/debug -> done

## live tmux (scratch repo notes/tmp/test/repo, .chaude/settings.json main_model=sonnet agent_wait=true language=ko; env CHAUDE_LANG=ko)
- prompt (ko): 2 background agents (sleep 40 README.md line count; sleep 60 notes entry count), plan paragraph first, one final reply after both return.
- run1 (session chaude-wait-test, now killed): plan text shown; status bar "에이전트 2개 대기 중" at k1_a; one final reply at k1_c (line 44) after both returned; task list 완료 x2; idle prompt. captures: notes/tmp/test/cap/k1_a.txt, k1_b.txt, k1_c.txt
- run2 (session chaude-wait-test-ko, killed): "대기 중" in k2_a; one final reply at k2_b line 45 after tui translation; task list 완료 x2; idle prompt. captures: cap/k2_a.txt, k2_b.txt
- run3 (session chaude-wait-ko, killed): "에이전트 2개 대기 중" in k3_a line 51; one final reply at k3_b line 42; task list 완료 x2; idle prompt. captures: cap/k3_a.txt, k3_b.txt
- all 3 runs: tmux sessions killed (tmux ls: no server). No kill-server.

## doubts
- not verified: the 3 new tests were not run against pre-fix code (fixes are uncommitted and mixed in api.rs, so no clean revert). They pass on current code only.
- pre-release main text blocks (● lines between tool calls, e.g. k2_b line 15, k1_b lines 5/19) stay in the transcript while held in all 3 runs. Only the continuation (tui answer) is the final reply; the spec allows text shown before release, but the user does see this text.
- haiku sub-agents again misreported: run1 and run2 needed SendMessage to re-request results ("결과당 보고 1회" limit rejected the second report); run1 agent claimed sleep still running, then corrected. Counting numbers differ across runs (README line count, notes entries). Not part of the wait check.

- failing list (all crates/core/tests/engine.rs): go_idle_spend_row_background_row, go_rotate_keeps_pending_snapshot, agent_pending_on_live_owned_task_is_not_ended_by_grace, agent_row_of_a_running_typed_agent_carries_type_and_model, agent_completion_waits_for_live_owned_task_then_ends_once, agent_completion_with_task_id_parent_waits_for_owned_task, agent_rows_carry_panel_fields_for_a_background_launch, background_agent_replay_has_no_agent_end_for_async_launch, done_notice_after_rotation_carries_brief, drained_agent_completion_is_delivered_once_and_terminal, drained_background_agent_times_out_with_notice_and_ends, drained_agent_done_summary_is_handback_message (+ others in the 15)
evidence:
- drained_agent_done_summary_is_handback_message: crates/core/tests/engine.rs:1231 "engine went silent: Timeout" after 66.36s; test waits for UiEvent::Rotated
- cause: hold_reply (api.rs ~3985) returns true when background Agent still running at end_turn; end_turn then skips finish_turn (where rotation runs), so no Rotated. Release (release_held ~3962) sends a continuation turn, which consumes a scripted fake-claude turn the fixtures do not expect.
- hold lasts until agent_wait_timeout (1800s default) if no completion arrives; explains multi-minute hangs (exit_handoff_* ok only after >60s)
hypothesis: pre-existing engine.rs fixtures encode old behavior (no hold on background launch). Brief's hold/continuation spec conflicts with them.
options:
- A: set agent_wait_timeout low in engine.rs config helper and adjust fixtures for the continuation turn (about 15 tests, crates/core/tests only)
- B: narrow hold rule (e.g. skip hold for drained/owned agents) — deviates from brief, needs main decision
risk: held turns delay TurnEnd/rotation by up to agent_wait_timeout; behavior change for all background launches
cleanup: rm -rf target/debug (done); no commit

- /agents is NOT an open command; it is sent to the main model as a prompt.

## goal1 quit delay
old_delay: quit path ran ClaudeProc::finish(3s) sequentially per live child (main, prompter, tui, each coder); each waited up to 3 s for stdin-EOF flush before kill. worst case = 3 s x live children.
fix: crates/core/src/api.rs quit path (~line 1407) calls p.kill() directly; grace removed. drains still killed after.
window_close_sighup: not handled (forward_signals covers SIGINT/SIGTERM only). not changed. doubt.

## goal2 /resume digest
new: crates/core/src/resume_note.rs
  build(imp, agents, cap) -> String: header, Agents rows (status/type/task -> out_path), Last recommendation (reco), Changed files, then Turns newest-first (user <=600 chars, reply tail <=1500 chars); result <= cap (12000 via RESUME_CAP).
  write(cwd, imp, sids) -> notes/tmp/<newest session id>.md; agents from ~/.chaude/agents.json via AgentStore::load(sid) (non-empty parent = all statuses).
  hint(path) -> "Read <path> first: it digests the previous session. Continue that prior work."
wired:
  import_claude.rs: ClaudeImport.note_path: Option<PathBuf>

.gitignore: added notes/tmp/
source: Claude transcript (~/.claude/projects/...) via read_claude_transcript; no agent tool capture there, agent rows only from agents.json.
killed_session: partial last line -> parsed as _unparsed, skipped; prior turns kept.

- Unconfirmed: which process created chaude.bak-<ver> files. Not in v0.12.0 install.sh, not in v0.11.10 cleanup.rs. No zsh_history hit. Running chaude processes (ps) are from /usr/local/bin and ~/.local/bin/claude sessions; no 0.11.10 process start time found.
- ps note: a background zsh loop (started 09:11) polls `~/.local/bin/chaude --version` for 0.12.0. Left untouched.

status: partial (groups a, b, c done; d1 done; d2, d3 open)
branch: rust-port
commits:
- 9fb41bc feat(core): add rotate helpers; port Go thresholdFor, shouldRotate, handoff validation
- 4817e82 feat(core): add Cmd::Usage and UiEvent::Usage; port Go /usage one-shot
- ad7b74d feat(core): add UiEvent::BudgetStatus, BudgetStop; port Go per-prompt budget
- 71864e3 fix(core): Go askPerm ask path with auto_allow; clear pending prompts on abandon
- 961f7f2 feat(core): add UiEvent::PermissionRequest, ModeChanged; port Go permMode and askPerm
- d7d4b8a (cli, not mine) fix(tui): busy row sits in the transcript flow like Go
- 570c129 (cli, not mine) fix(tui): Go single-press Esc interrupt while busy
- be19a41 fix(core): end interrupted turn after interrupt_grace; Config.interrupt_grace (20s)
- d726eb2 fix(core): discard stray result of an abandoned turn
- 6f3da24 feat(core): mark UiEvent #[non_exhaustive]; cli apply gets fallback arm
- b430929 feat(core): add UiEvent::ToolResult, AgentBackground, Interrupted
- 22066b1 feat(core): add UiEvent::RateLimit { info }
- 9861089 feat(core): add UiEvent::TurnStats { duration_ms, cost_turn_usd, cost_usd, ctx, delegations }

## a. risk fixes (71864e3)
- AutoAllow: Config.auto_allow; Ask with auto_allow allows without a prompt (Go askPerm).
- modeLine with no args is handled by the cli; core still has set_mode("next") for cycling.
- Idle deny: unreachable in Rust (engine always has a UI channel). Documented.
- Pending prompts are cleared on abandon (turn ended without answer).

## b. budget (ad7b74d)
- crates/core/src/budget.rs: Budget {cap, spent, running, last per proc}. charge books the delta per process (drop = whole total), reset per prompt keeps baselines, charge_one_shot, allow(est), over(running). estimate() (warm read / cache write 5m or 1h, plus 4k output) and mid_turn_estimate(usage). Price table ported from Go budget.go; alias via price_key (full ids claude-opus-5-5 etc).
- Config.task_budget_usd (0 = off). SpawnOpts.max_budget_usd -> proc::budget_args -> --max-budget-usd backstop.
- send_turn: reset, then estimate_next + allow(est). Refused: UiEvent::BudgetStop, no user frame written, returns false.
- Mid-turn: a main assistant usage event whose estimate reaches cap -> one write_interrupt (budget_hit), interrupted_at set. No Cmd::Interrupt, so no interrupt log line (Go does not log these either).
- Result: charge CHILD_PROC then UiEvent::BudgetStatus { spent_usd, cap_usd }.
- end_turn: UiEvent::BudgetStop before TurnStats when budget_hit.
- Differences from Go: warm-cache window uses cache TTL caps (3600 s for "1h", else 290 s) because Rust has no idle-rotate setting. Resume baseline is 0 (same as Go resumeBaseline).
- Tests: budget.rs unit x7 (delta_per_process, cap_zero_is_off, reset_clears_spent_and_keeps_baseline, resumed_process_books_whole_first_total, allow_and_over_against_cap, full_model_id_is_priced_like_alias, unknown_model_costs_nothing); proc::budget_args_only_when_cap_is_set; engine: budget_interrupts_turn_once_when_in_flight_estimate_reaches_cap, budget_refuses_prompt_whose_next_call_estimate_exceeds_cap, budget_status_reports_spent_and_cap_after_each_turn. Fixture: tests/fixtures/budget_turn.jsonl.
- Gates (ad7b74d):
  - cargo fmt --all -- --check -> PASS
  - cargo clippy -p chaude-core --all-targets -- -D warnings -> PASS
  - cargo test -p chaude-core -> PASS (unit 37, engine 22)
  - cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -D warnings -> FAIL, not core: crates/cli/src/tui/commands.rs:163 (PermMode::next unused), :190 (UPDATE_START_NOTE unused). TUI coder WIP, not touched.

## c. engine-side slash commands (4817e82)
- Scope check: /status, /version, /mode, /model, /help are answered by the cli TUI from its own state (Go answers them in tui.go/localcmd.go, not via the child). Core needs no events for them. /mode (empty name, "next", names) is core set_mode. /budget is not a Go command.
- Only real gap: /usage. Go usageRaw runs `claude -p /usage --output-format json --no-session-persistence` in the temp dir with cleanEnv and returns JSON `result`.
- crates/core/src/proc.rs one_shot_usage(bin): same env as the session (apply_env with compact 0, cache_ttl ""), stdin/stderr null. Returns result text or "usage: ..." error.
- api.rs: Cmd::Usage, EngineHandle::usage(), UiEvent::Usage { text }. State::usage spawns a thread so a busy turn keeps streaming; the thread sends Usage or Error.
- Not charged to the budget. Go never calls chargeOneShot (budget.go:46), so mirroring Go means no charge. Budget::charge_one_shot stays unused.
- Known quirk, same as Go: the session child and the one-shot both use Config.claude_bin.
- Tests (engine.rs): usage_answers_with_result_text_from_one_shot_run (wrapper script: `-p /usage` prints the result, other argv execs the fake session), usage_missing_binary_reports_error. usage_reply skips session spawn errors because Engine::start spawns the session child too.

<!-- notes-gc: notes/runs/issue2-reply.md (mtime 2026-11-03) -->
원인을 찾아 0.9.14에서 수정했습니다.

**원인:** 메인 모델이 Bash·파일 도구를 직접 쓰지 못하게 막는 훅은 작업 폴더의 `.claude/settings.json`에 등록되어 있을 때만 동작했습니다. chaude 저장소가 아닌 폴더에는 이 설정이 없어서 막는 장치가 꺼져 있었습니다.

**수정:** 세션을 시작할 때 작업 폴더에 `.claude/settings.json`이 없으면, 실행 파일에 포함된 훅으로 설정과 훅 파일을 자동으로 만듭니다. 이미 있는 설정은 건드리지 않고, 홈 폴더에서는 만들지 않습니다.

`chaude update` 또는 설치 스크립트로 0.9.14로 업데이트해 주세요. Windows에서 겪으신 분은 업데이트 후에도 같은 증상이 나는지 알려 주시면 감사하겠습니다.
